`packages/server/src/import/zip.ts`:
```ts
export interface ZipEntry { name: string; data: Uint8Array }
export function readZip(buffer: Uint8Array): Map<string, Uint8Array>
export function readZipEntry(buffer, name): Uint8Array | null
export function isZip(buffer): boolean  // PK\x03\x04
```
Implementation: find EOCD signature `0x06054b50` scanning backwards from end (within last 64 KB), read central directory offset & count; iterate CD entries (sig 0x02014b50): compressionMethod, compressedSize, uncompressedSize, nameLength, extraLength, commentLength, localHeaderOffset; read local header (sig 0x04034b50) to get its name/extra lengths; data offset = localOffset + 30 + nameLen + extraLen; method 0 → copy; method 8 → inflateRawSync.

Handle Zip64? docx files < 4 GB, fine. Pure functions.

`packages/server/src/import/docx.ts`:
```ts
export function docxToText(buffer: Uint8Array): string
```
- read `word/document.xml`; if missing → throw ImportError('docx-结构不对'...).
- Decode UTF-8.
- Replace `<w:p ...>` boundaries: simplest: split on `</w:p>`; within each paragraph, collect text from `<w:t...>(.*?)</w:t>`, unescape XML entities, handle `<w:tab/>` → '\t', `<w:br/>` → '\n'. Also handle `w:instrText` (field codes) — skip them (they're in `<w:instrText>` tags, not `<w:t>`).
- Trim and drop empty paragraphs → join lines with '\n'. Also should we keep empty lines? Resume structure benefits from blank line separation, but empty paragraphs add noise. I'll collapse runs of blank lines to a single blank line, preserving paragraph structure.

XML entity decode: &amp; &lt; &gt; &quot; &apos; &#NN; &#xHH;.

Also handle `<w:t xml:space="preserve">`.

```ts
export async function pdfToText(buffer: Uint8Array): Promise<string>
```
using unpdf: getDocumentProxy + extractText mergePages; then normalize: collapse 3+ newlines; trim. If text length < 10 → throw ImportError with scanned-PDF hint.

```ts
const IMPORT_FORM_FIELD = 'file';

app.post('/api/import', async (c) => {
  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try {
    body = await c.req.parseBody();
  } catch (error) {
    return c.json({ error: '这个上传请求读不出来：' + messageOf(error) }, 400);
  }
  const file = body[IMPORT_FORM_FIELD];
  if (!(file instanceof File)) {
    return c.json({ error: '没有收到文件。请选择一份 .pdf / .docx / .txt 简历再试。' }, 400);
  }
  if (file.size > MAX_IMPORT_BYTES) { ... } // handled by extract too but cheap early check
  try {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
    const result = await extractResumeFromFile({ name: file.name, bytes });
    return c.json({ ...result }, 200);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ImportError) return c.json({ error: error.message, code: error.code }, statusForImport(error.code));
    return c.json({ error: '导入失败：' + messageOf(error) }, 500);
  }
});
```

```ts
  /**
   * 简历导入：把用户手上的 PDF / Word 变成一段文字。
   * 上传的对象**不落库、不留盘**：解出文字返回给前端，用户看清了再决定要不要拿它开跑。
   */
  app.post('/api/import', async (c) => {
    let body: Record<string, unknown>;
    try {
      body = (await c.req.parseBody()) as Record<string, unknown>;
    } catch (err) {
      return c.json({ error: '这个上传请求读不出来：' + messageOf(err) }, 400);
    }
    const file = body[IMPORT_FIELD];
    if (!(file instanceof File)) {
      return c.json({ error: '没有收到文件。挑一份 ' + IMPORT_ACCEPT + ' 简历再试。' }, 400);
    }
    try {
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const imported = await extractResumeFromFile({ name: file.name, bytes });
      return c.json(imported);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ImportError) {
        return c.json({ error: err.message, code: err.code }, importStatus(err.code));
      }
      return c.json({ error: '导入失败：' + messageOf(err) }, 500);
    }
  });
```

with helpers:

```ts
const IMPORT_FIELD = 'file';

function importStatus(code: ImportErrorCode): 413 | 415 | 422 {
  if (code === 'too-large') return 413;
  if (code === 'unsupported' || code === 'legacy-doc') return 415;
  return 422;
}
```

Type: `ContentfulStatusCode` in Hono types — c.json(obj, status) requires a valid status type. 413/415/422 are fine.

Now web side: api.ts add `importResume(file: File): Promise<ImportedResume>`; types `ImportedResume { kind, name, text, chars, notes }`.

api.ts: add after createSession maybe. Need FormData POST; error handling via readError.

```ts
export interface ImportedResume {
  kind: 'pdf' | 'docx' | 'text';
  name: string;
  text: string;
  chars: number;
  notes: string[];
}

export async function importResumeFile(file: File): Promise<ImportedResume> {
  const form = new FormData();
  form.append('file', file);
  const res = await fetch('/api/import', { method: 'POST', body: form });
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(await readError(res), res.status);
  return (await res.json()) as ImportedResume;
}
```

Now IntakePanel: add file input + button + status line. Props: add `onImportFile: (file: File) => void;` and `importHint: string | null;` — importHint being a status line (success or error). Let me design: App keeps `importState: {tone:'ok'|'error', text:string} | null` deriving from `importHint`? Simpler: pass `importHint: string | null` and `importError: boolean`? I'll use `importNote: { tone: 'ok' | 'error'; text: string } | null`.

In IntakePanel label for 简历正文: the header span has "简历正文" and char count. I'll add next to the char count a small "从文件导入" button + hidden file input. Actually the label wraps the textarea; putting a button inside a label means clicking the button triggers the label's default behavior (focus textarea) — that's fine but the file input click needs `event.preventDefault()`? Clicking a button inside a label: label activation would forward click to the labelled control. Since the label contains multiple form controls (button, input file, textarea), label's associated control is the first labelable descendant — wait, the first labelable descendant of the label here is... the textarea is the first. Hmm, actually a label with a button inside: the button is a labelable element? Per HTML spec, labelable elements: button, input, meter, output, progress, select, textarea. So the first labelable descendant is whichever appears first in DOM. If I put the file button before the textarea, the label's control becomes the button → clicking anywhere in the label triggers the button (opening file dialog). Bad.

Safer: restructure — take the header row out of the `<label>` and make it a div, then use `<label>` only wrapping the textarea, or use htmlFor/id. Let me restructure the 简历正文 block:

```tsx

        从文件导入
      </button>
    </span>
  </span>
  <textarea id="resume-text" ... />
  {props.importNote === null ? null : (

  )}
</div>
```

File input: App holds a hidden input ref? That keeps App simpler: IntakePanel owns the input and calls `props.onImportFile(file)`.

```tsx
const handleImportFile = useCallback((file: File) => {

    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, resumeText: imported.text }));

  }).catch((error: unknown) => {

  });
}, []);
```

Actually pdf.js tolerates broken xref (it reconstructs). A minimal PDF:

```
%PDF-1.4
1 0 obj << /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >> endobj
2 0 obj << /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >> endobj
3 0 obj << /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 200 200] /Contents 4 0 R >> endobj
4 0 obj << /Length 0 >> stream
endstream endobj
trailer << /Root 1 0 R >>
%%EOF
```
pdf.js without xref: it may warn "Invalid PDF structure" but often recovers by scanning objects. Risky but we can test and see. If it fails, error code would be pdf-broken instead of pdf-no-text — my test can assert "either pdf-no-text or pdf-broken"? That weakens. Now write the test file. Cases:
1. docx fixture → exact text, kind 'docx', chars === text.length, notes empty.
2. zip broken: bytes = Uint8Array of text "not a zip" with name 'x.docx' → ImportError code 'broken'.
3. pdf fixture (resume-edge.pdf) → kind 'pdf', text includes '李雷' and some line, notes.length === 1, chars>100.
4. handwritten no-text PDF → 'pdf-no-text' (test and see).
5. broken pdf (garbage bytes with .pdf) → 'pdf-broken'.
6. legacy doc: bytes starting with D0CF11E0A1B11AE1 + zeros, name 'x.doc' → 'legacy-doc'.
7. unsupported: name 'photo.png' with text bytes → 'unsupported'.
8. empty file → 'empty'.
9. too-large: maxBytes 10 with valid docx bytes → 'too-large' (message has MB).
10. GBK text: Buffer.from('张三\n后端工程师\n', 'utf8')? Need GBK bytes: use iconv? No iconv. Hardcode GBK bytes for a Chinese string, e.g. '简历' in GBK = 0xBC 0xF2 0xC0 0xFA. So bytes = Uint8Array [0xBC,0xF2,0xC0,0xFA,0x0A] with name 'resume.txt' → text should be '简历'. UTF-8 decode of those bytes → replacement chars ratio 1.0 → then gbk → '简历'. Good.
11. UTF-8 text import + normalizeImportedText folding: raw 'a\n\n\nb\n  \nc   \n\n' → 'a\n\nb\n\nc'. Let me compute: split lines: 'a','','','b','  ','c   ','',''. Line trailing whitespace stripped: 'a','','','b','','c','',''. Kept logic: 'a' → kept ['a']; '' → last is 'a' not '' → push '' → ['a','']; '' → last is '' → skip; 'b' → push; '' → push; '' → skip; 'c' → push; '' → push; '' skip; '' skip. Then trailing '' popped → ['a','','b','','c'] → 'a\n\nb\n\nc'. Yes.
12. looksLikeLegacyDoc magic detection and importKindOf/extensionOf units.
13. text with only whitespace → 'empty' ("这个文件里没有可用的文字。").

Interesting: the first call `codeOf('scan.pdf', bytes)` returned 'pdf-no-text' (passed). But the second call `extractResumeFromFile` got '这个文件是空的。' — that's odd. Because `.rejects.toThrow` — wait, the error is '这个文件是空的。' meaning bytes.byteLength === 0? That means `bytes` was consumed/mutated! unpdf's getDocumentProxy with a Uint8Array may transfer/detach the buffer? Actually pdf.js may transfer the underlying ArrayBuffer to a worker, detaching it → byteLength becomes 0.

So calling twice on the same Uint8Array fails. That's a real behavioral note: unpdf detaches the input buffer. Important for the server: we call it once, fine. But worth documenting (F-note).

Fix test: create fresh bytes for the second call. Also worth asserting this behavior? Better to note it and add a regression assertion that a second call on the same buffer... hmm, that's an unpdf implementation detail. <details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

The blank file case: `new TextEncoder().encode('   ') + ' '` — that's a string concat resulting in a string, and File([string]) works... The route: bytes = Uint8Array of the file. Text '    ' (4 spaces) → decodePlainText → '    ' → normalizeImportedText: lines ['    '→ line trailing whitespace stripped → ''], kept: '' push? kept.length===0 and line.trim()==='' → skip. So kept empty → text '' → trim length 0 → empty error. Should be 422.

But got 200. Why? Maybe `File([...])` with the string '    ' — the file's size... Hmm wait, `new File([new TextEncoder().encode('   ') + ' '], 'blank.txt')` → the array part is a Uint8Array plus string, giving "32,32,32,32 " ... Actually `String(Uint8Array)` = "32,32,32" — concatenating a Uint8Array with a string coerces the array to a string "32,32,32" then + " " = "32,32,32 ". So the file content is the ASCII text "32,32,32 " which is not empty → 200. My bug: string coercion.

Fix: `new File([new TextEncoder().encode('   ')], 'blank.txt')`.

Wait, but then it's 3 spaces → empty code. Good.

</details>

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

TypeScript infers `result.text` as string because with `mergePages: true` the overload returns text: string. So the ternary's false branch is `never`. Fix: trust the typed overload — `text = result.text;` and keep pages. But runtime safety for mergePages false? We always pass true. Simplify:

```ts
export async function pdfToText(buffer: Uint8Array): Promise<string> {
  let text: string;
  let pages: number;
  try {
    const document = await getDocumentProxy(buffer);
    const result = await extractText(document, { mergePages: true });
    text = result.text;
    pages = result.totalPages;
  } catch ...
```

But `result.text` typing: overload with mergePages: true gives `{ totalPages: number; text: string }`. Good.

Yet defensive: if a future unpdf returns array, typeof check on a `string`-typed var is flagged. Just do the direct assignment.

</details>

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

verify exit 0: 36 test files / 360 assertions, smoke 9, build ok.

Now: e2e:ui import scenario + shots. Import in a browser e2e requires setting a file input's files — via CDP `DOM.setFileInputFiles` (needs node id) or `Input.insertText`? Standard approach: `DOM.getDocument`, `DOM.querySelector`, `DOM.setFileInputFiles`. Alternatively use `DataTransfer` in JS: create a File in page context and set input.files — that works in Chrome: 
```js
const dt = new DataTransfer(); dt.items.add(new File([...], 'resume.md', {type:'text/plain'})); input.files = dt.files; input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', {bubbles:true}));
```
React's onChange listens to 'change' (React uses 'change' for file inputs via its own event system, but native dispatch with bubbles works since React 17 attaches at root and maps change → onChange for inputs). For file inputs React uses 'change' event. Yes, dispatching a bubbling 'change' triggers React's onChange... Actually React attaches 'change' listener for file inputs? React's onChange for text inputs listens to 'input'. For file inputs and checkbox/radio, React's onChange maps to 'click'/'change'. Known recipe: dispatching `new Event('change', {bubbles:true})` after setting files works with React.

But `input.files = dt.files` — DataTransfer constructor is available in Chrome. Good.

So in e2e.mjs, add a scenario: build a text file in-page, set it on `[data-shot=import-file]`, dispatch change, wait for `[data-shot=import-note]` text contains '解析出' and textarea value contains the content.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let me implement the fixes in both tools:

Shared approach (duplicated per tool, zero deps):
```js
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync, ... } from 'node:fs';

/** Windows 上 proc.kill() 只结束启动器，Edge 的进程树还活着；
 *  残留的浏览器会继续占着 CDP 端口，下一次跑就会连到它——而它的 profile 里
 *  「教程看过了」之类的状态会让断言假失败。所以按进程树杀。 */
function killTree(proc) {
  if (proc === null || proc.pid === undefined) return;
  try {
    if (process.platform === 'win32') spawnSync('taskkill', ['/pid', String(proc.pid), '/T', '/F'], { stdio: 'ignore' });
    else proc.kill('SIGKILL');
  } catch { /* 已经退出了 */ }
}

async function portInUse(port) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + String(port) + '/json/version');
    return res.ok;
  } catch { return false; }
}
```
And before spawning the browser: 
```js
if (await portInUse(CDP_PORT)) { log('调试端口 ... 已被占用：多半是上一次留下的浏览器进程。先关掉它（taskkill /F /IM msedge.exe 只杀调试实例更稳）'); process.exit(1); }
```
Hmm, but a plain TCP check is better than /json/version (any listener). Use /json/version for specificity, plus a raw TCP attempt? Keep /json/version: if something else listens there, our CDP connection would fail anyway with a clear error.

Actually more robust: pick another port automatically if occupied? No — silently changing ports hides the problem. Fail loudly is better, with the fix instruction.

Also after connecting, verify the target is our fresh window:
```js
if (pageTarget.url !== 'about:blank') throw new Error('连到的不是刚启动的浏览器窗口（当前页面 ' + pageTarget.url + '）……');
```
And check `/json/version` browser string? Not needed.

```
let serverProc = null;
let edgeProc = null;
let cleaned = false;
function cleanup() {
  if (cleaned) return;
```

with added helpers. Let me write:

```
let serverProc = null;
let edgeProc = null;
let cleaned = false;
// Windows 上 proc.kill() 只结束启动器，Edge 的进程树还活着。残留的浏览器会继续占着
// CDP 端口，下一次跑就悄悄连到它——而它的 profile 里「教程看过了」之类的状态会让断言
// 假失败（真的踩过：9344 端口上挂着两个旧浏览器）。所以按进程树杀。
function killTree(proc) {
  if (proc === null || proc.pid === undefined) return;
  try {
    if (process.platform === 'win32') spawnSync('taskkill', ['/pid', String(proc.pid), '/T', '/F'], { stdio: 'ignore' });
    else proc.kill('SIGKILL');
  } catch (error) { /* 已经退出了 */ }
}
function cleanup() {
  if (cleaned) return;
  cleaned = true;
  killTree(edgeProc);
  killTree(serverProc);
}
```

Edit 3: before spawning edge, replace:

```
edgeProc = spawn(EDGE, [...]);
```
with a check:

```
// 连上去就等于用别人的 profile 做断言（教程状态、历史会话都不是这一轮的）。
let cdpBusy = false;
try { const r = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + String(CDP_PORT) + '/json/version'); cdpBusy = r.ok; } catch (e) { /* 空的 */ }
if (cdpBusy) {
  console.log('调试端口 ' + String(CDP_PORT) + ' 已被占用：多半是上一轮留下的浏览器进程。');
  console.log('先清掉：taskkill /F /IM msedge.exe（或按端口找 PID 再杀），再重跑。');
  process.exit(1);
}
edgeProc = spawn(...)
```

Instead change the find predicate: `const page = list.find((target) => target.type === 'page' && target.url === 'about:blank');` and error message mentions if a page exists with a different url. That's clean:

```
async function waitForDevtools(port, timeoutMs) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
  let lastError = 'unknown';
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    try {
      const list = await (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + String(port) + '/json/list')).json();
      // 只认「刚启动的空窗口」。如果端口上已经有别的页面，说明连到的是别人留下的浏览器，
      // 它的 profile 里可能已经有 localStorage（例如「教程看过了」），会让断言假失败。
      const page = list.find((target) => target.type === 'page' && target.url === 'about:blank');
      if (page !== undefined && typeof page.webSocketDebuggerUrl === 'string') return page.webSocketDebuggerUrl;
      const stranger = list.find((target) => target.type === 'page');
      lastError = stranger === undefined ? '还没有 page target' : '端口上的页面是 ' + String(stranger.url) + '，不是刚启动的空窗口';
    } catch (error) {
      lastError = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
    }
    await sleep(250);
  }
  throw new Error('等不到浏览器调试端口 ' + String(port) + '：' + lastError);
}
```

Unless the spawn used the msedge.exe launcher which created a *different* process and exited — the docs: msedge.exe spawns and the started process may be a new one; on Windows, Edge's executable performs a "browser broker" dance.

The earlier orphan kill test showed 57 processes, many with `do-not-de-elevate about:blank` — those are the ones that linger.

Better fix: after killing by pid, also verify port free and, as last resort, kill by port owner / by profile path match. A robust zero-dep approach: 
1. `killTree(proc)` via taskkill.
2. After that, in the finally, if the debug port is still occupied → find PIDs whose command line matches the unique profile dir and taskkill them (using pwsh? no — use `wmic`? deprecated). Zero-dep on Windows without pwsh: could use `taskkill /F /IM msedge.exe` — but that would kill the user's own browsing Edge! Not acceptable.

Alternative that avoids the whole problem: **track the actual browser PID via CDP**. `GET /json/version` returns `webSocketDebuggerUrl` for the browser target; we can connect to the browser endpoint and use `Browser.getVersion`... but that doesn't give the PID. However `SystemInfo.getProcessInfo` (CDP Browser domain) returns process info with `id` (not OS pid). Hmm.

Better: make our spawned process the one that matters. Instead of relying on proc.pid, at cleanup use the CDP connection: `Browser.close` — CDP has `Browser.close` which gracefully closes the whole browser! That's the clean, correct approach: call `Browser.close` on the browser-level WebSocket URL (`/json/version`'s webSocketDebuggerUrl), then fall back to killTree.

Even simpler: `Target.closeTarget`. But `Browser.close` is exactly right.

So in cleanup: can't do async easily in exit handler... but we can do it *before* exit in the main flow (in the finally block), which shots.mjs and e2e.mjs both have (shots has finally; e2e has no finally — cleanup only).

Now, implement a robust teardown that actually closes the browser via CDP `Browser.close`.

Add to e2e.mjs (after `const ws = new WebSocket(...)` and connection helpers, i.e., near send) a function:

```js
// 收尾要真的把浏览器关掉。proc.kill() 在 Windows 上只结束启动器，Edge 的浏览器进程
// 会活下来并继续占着调试端口——下一轮就会连到它，断言基于别人的 profile 假通过/假失败。
// CDP 的 Browser.close 才是「关掉这个浏览器」的正解；taskkill /T /F 只当兜底。
async function closeBrowser() {
  try {
    const info = await (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + String(CDP_PORT) + '/json/version')).json();
    const browserWs = new WebSocket(info.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
    await new Promise((res, rej) => { browserWs.addEventListener('open', res); browserWs.addEventListener('error', rej); setTimeout(rej, 5000); });
    browserWs.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: 'Browser.close' }));
    await sleep(800);
    try { browserWs.close(); } catch (e) {}
    return true;
  } catch (error) { return false; }
}
```
Careful with `setTimeout(rej, 5000)` — an unhandled rejection later; wrap. Simpler: use a helper promise with timeout that resolves rather than rejects.

Then at line 387: `await closeBrowser(); cleanup();`

```js
async function ensurePortFree(port, label) {
  let busy = false;
  try { const r = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + String(port) + '/json/version'); busy = r.ok; } catch (e) { }
  if (busy) console.log('警告：' + label + ' ' + String(port) + ' 还被占着，残留的浏览器会干扰下一轮；找到占用者：Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort ' + String(port) + ' -State Listen');
}
```

For shots.mjs closeBrowser:
```js
// 收尾要真的把浏览器关掉：proc.kill() 在 Windows 上只结束启动器，Edge 的浏览器进程会活
// 下来并继续占着调试端口，下一轮就可能连到它（它的 profile 里已经有 localStorage）。
// CDP 的 Browser.close 才是「关掉这个浏览器」的正解，taskkill 只当兜底。
async function closeBrowser(port) {
  try {
    const response = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + String(port) + '/json/version');
    if (!response.ok) return false;
    const info = await response.json();
    if (typeof info.webSocketDebuggerUrl !== 'string') return false;
    const socket = new WebSocket(info.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
    await new Promise((done) => {
      const timer = setTimeout(done, 5000);
      socket.addEventListener('open', () => { clearTimeout(timer); done(); });
      socket.addEventListener('error', () => { clearTimeout(timer); done(); });
    });
    socket.send(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method: 'Browser.close' }));
    await sleep(700);
    try { socket.close(); } catch { /* 已经关了 */ }
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

// 关完再确认一次：端口还占着就明确说出来，别让下一轮静默连到别人。
async function warnIfPortBusy(port) {
  try {
    const response = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + String(port) + '/json/version');
    if (response.ok) {
      log('警告：调试端口 ' + String(port) + ' 仍被占用，残留浏览器会干扰下一轮。');
      log('  找占用者：Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort ' + String(port) + ' -State Listen');
    }
  } catch { /* 空的，正常 */ }
}
```

Same for e2e (console.log instead of log).

Then rerun e2e and verify no orphans + run shots too.

Note: after `Browser.close`, the port should free within ~1s. We sleep 700ms then warn. Maybe sleep 900. Fine.

Let's implement.

```text
# ADR-0006：报告导出为三种形态，服务端不自己生成 PDF

- 状态：已采纳
- 日期：2026-10-26
- 相关：ADR-0001（服务端零构建）、ADR-0003（单端口托管前端）

## 背景

质询跑完，用户拿到的是屏幕上的一堆卡片。但一份报告真正的用途在屏幕之外：
发给同学看、打印出来对着改简历、贴进复盘文档。赛题评分里有「解题有效性与实用价值 25%」，
一个只能在线看的结论，价值会打折扣。

问题的难点不在排版，而在**谁来生成 PDF**。

## 决策

1. **服务端只出 HTML，不出 PDF。** 生成 PDF 意味着服务端要带一个无头浏览器
   （或一个 PDF 库），前者会把「10 分钟复现」变成赌评委机器上装了什么，
   后者要么是原生依赖（违反零原生依赖），要么自己造排版引擎（代价远超收益）。
   改成：服务端渲染一份**自带打印样式**的 HTML，`?print=1` 时页面 load 后自动调 `window.print()`，
   剩下的交给浏览器——用户按「另存为 PDF」即可，这条路在任何装了浏览器的地方都成立。
2. **Word 用「同一份 HTML + `.doc` 后缀 + `application/msword`」。** 这是 Word 至今仍认的老格式，
   零依赖、可编辑、打开即排版。代价是没有 `.docx` 的严格结构（见「代价」）。
3. **三种形态同源。** `renderReport`（Markdown）与 `renderReportHtml`（HTML）吃的是同一个 `SessionView`，
   章节顺序一一对应（过程说明 / 主张与判定 / 追问实录 / 修改建议 / 复现方式），
   谁也不会多出对方没有的结论。
4. **不复用 `tools/docs/md2html.mjs`。** 那个渲染器服务于技术文档（工具目录、只认 Markdown 子集），
   而报告需要锚点引用块、判定配色与打印分页控制——它是产品的一部分，不是文档工具的一部分。
5. **HTML 报告不引用任何外部资源**（无 CDN、无字体文件、无脚本外链），
   保存单文件即可离线打开；页脚写明「简历与报告都没有离开这台机器」。

## 备选方案

- **服务端用 headless 浏览器出 PDF**：能得到最漂亮的 PDF，但把复现前提从「装了 Node」变成
  「装了 Node 且能找到浏览器」，与 README 的十分钟承诺冲突。
- **引入 `pdfkit` / `puppeteer`**：前者是纯 JS 但需自己排版分页，后者是原生依赖。均被零依赖约束挡下。
- **只提供 Markdown**：携带性最好，但评委/用户第一眼看到的是带 `#` 和 `|
…（此处省略 989 字符，原始长度 2189）
```

```js
## 背景

用户提出「支持 doc 和 pdf 导入简历」（m04531 第 2 项）。看着是一件小事，实际上它把
产品的入口从「你得会复制粘贴」变成「选一个文件」，而这一项的门槛决定了非计算机专业的
用户能不能用起来。难点全在**解析**：一份简历可能是 PDF（文字版或扫描件）、.docx、
老版 .doc、纯文本。

这里有一条不能破的约束：评委要在 10 分钟内把整条链路跑起来。任何需要本地编译的
原生依赖、或任何体积巨大且带可选原生模块的库，都会把这条承诺变成赌博。

## 决策

1. **PDF 用 unpdf。** 它是纯 JS、零依赖（内联了自己裁剪过的 pdf.js 构建），
   npm 安装即用，不需要任何本地编译。对比过的 pdfjs-dist 有 34.9 MB，
   而且带着可选原生依赖 @napi-rs/canvas。取文字只需要 extractText，unpdf 正好够。
```

```bash
cd /d/CZB
count() { # $1 = pathspec, $2 = filter regexp (grep -E), $3 = exclude regexp optional
  files=$(git ls-files "$1" | grep -E "$2" | { [ -n "$3" ] && grep -vE "$3" || cat; })
  [ -z "$files" ] && echo 0 && return

}
```

Append two rows after.

D31 | 简历导入只做「解析成文本」，绝不落库留盘 | 导入的价值是把内容喂进已有链路，而不是新增一条数据通路；字节不落盘（连临时文件都不写）意味着「简历没有离开这台机器」这句话在导入路径上同样成立；PDF 用 unpdf 一个纯 JS 依赖而不是 pdfjs-dist（后者 34.9 MB 且带原生可选依赖 @napi-rs/canvas）——「十分钟复现」不允许赌评委机器；见 ADR-0007 |

D32 | 浏览器脚本的收尾必须由 CDP `Browser.close` 完成，并且开跑前先预检调试端口 | Windows 上 Edge 的启动器进程先退出，`proc.kill()`/`taskkill <pid>` 找不到真正的浏览器进程树；端口被上一轮的孤儿占着时，新脚本会连到旧浏览器（它的 localStorage 里已经写着「教程看过了」），于是断言假失败而根因不在被测代码；见 F40 |

Content:
### 3.6.4 简历导入：解析成文本，但不留档

`POST /api/import` 只做一件事：把上传的字节变成简历正文，填进投料台输入框，然后**字节就被丢掉**——
不写临时文件、不入库、不进日志。所以「简历没有离开这台机器」在导入这条路径上同样成立（上传的字节连磁盘都不落）。

解析按格式分三条路，每条都尽量避免依赖：PDF 用 `unpdf`（纯 JS，零原生编译），`.docx` 自己解 zip
（`node:zlib` 的 `inflateRawSync` 拆 `word/document.xml`，不引 SDK），纯文本先按 UTF-8 解、
解出乱码再试 GBK（中文简历的现实情况）。老版 `.doc` 是二进制复合文档，明确拒绝并提示另存为 `.docx`——
**宁可给一句可执行的话，也不要给一段看起来像正文的乱码**。取舍与实测教训见 ADR-0007、findings F39。

Also maybe mention it's covered by tests (11 assertions + 4 http).

- POST /api/sessions body: { resumeText, jdText, jobTitle, useDemo, mode?, model? } → server validates mode ∈ {live,replay,record}; picks client; stores llmMode = that mode; returns { id, mode, model, jobTitle }.
- Then run uses the session's stored mode: in run handler, if session.llmMode differs from the app-level client's mode (or if a model override was stored?), create the right client.

Hmm, we need to store the chosen client per session so the run uses it. Options:
(a) run endpoint accepts mode/model query too, and web passes them; DB stores llmMode for the record. Duplication risk (the "same truth in two places" issue).
(b) server keeps a per-session client (Map<sessionId, LlmClient>) — stateful, but app instances are per-process; the session row is durable. After a server restart, resume wouldn't have a client → fallback to app default. SessionView includes mode so the report says which mode ran.
(c) run reads session.llmMode/model from DB and constructs the client then — single source of truth (DB), no in-memory map. This is clean: POST /api/sessions stores mode + model in the session row; POST run builds the client from the stored session. Need a `model` column — or store in meta_json.
